| `qc.plate_effect` | `enhance.flatten_field` (corrects illumination WITHIN an image). This is bias ACROSS wells/positions. |
| `qc.psf_metrics` | detect.spots and detect.particles can find the beads and should be reused; neither fits a profile or reports FWHM. enhance.deconvolve derives a theoretical PSF from optics metadata - this node measures the real one, and should be able to hand it to deconvolve as a measured PSF, which is a genuinely new capability for that node. |
| `qc.resolution` | none. |
| `qc.tracking_accuracy` | Sibling of analysis.tracking_metrics; different metric family, different matching (point-gated vs mask-overlap), applicable where the other is not. |
| `registration.chromatic` | registration.stabilize registers over TIME on a reference channel (translation/euclidean/affine/feature) and applies to all channels; it cannot fit a per-channel model across c and has no scale-plus-radial-distortion model. registration.align_to compares stage logs between datasets. |
| `registration.deformable` | analysis.dic_correlate and analysis.dvc_field already produce non-rigid displacement fields, but they are measurement nodes with a correlation-window model, not regularized registration, and the pipeline has no consumer that warps a dataset by an arbitrary field. Note: a companion transform.warp_by_field node may be the cheaper first step and would also make the existing DIC/DVC outputs applicable. |
| `registration.template_match` | registration.stabilize likely does whole-frame correlation; this anchors on a chosen patch and reports a per-frame score. The locate_objects mode overlaps detect.particles only in output type - the detection principle (learned appearance versus scale-space blob) is different, and it works on non-blob shapes. |
| `registration.warp_model` | registration.stabilize registers once on a reference channel and applies to all channels; registration.align_to measures a stage-log offset by phase correlation and records it; align.drift is rigid drift over t; transform.rigid translates and rotates pixels in a fixed frame. All four are rigid/translational; none can express a projective or radial-distortion warp. |
| `relate.parent` | transform.transfer_structure exposes bridges.points_in_label (POINT->LABEL with a reducer) — that is the child=POINT case only, and it writes a value, not a persistent parent_id column. There is NO LABEL->LABEL bridge, so labelled-child-in-labelled-parent is unreachable, as is the reverse broadcast of a parent property onto its children. |
| `stats.batch_correct` | `table.normalize_column` per-group z-scoring is the degenerate case of this and should be offered there; ComBat's shrinkage across features is what this adds. |
| `stats.bootstrap_ci` | `stats.describe` reports a parametric CI of the mean; this is the distribution-free and hierarchically-correct version. |
| `stats.circular` | `analysis.object_metrics` and the DVC nodes produce vectors/angles; nothing tests or summarizes them circularly. |
| `stats.cluster` | analysis.cluster_points — this IS that node with two more entries in its method Mode, plus a noise label the existing modes have no concept of. |
| `stats.curve_fit` | flow.iterate does golden/secant search over a PARAMETER of the graph, not a least-squares fit of measured data. enhance.temporal_gain has an internal exponential_fit for bleach gain, hard-wired to that one purpose. |
| `stats.effect_size` | `stats.hypothesis_test` (p-values only). Pairs with it; could be a second output of that node, but is meaningful without any test. |
| `stats.feature_selection` | `stats.correlation` (redundancy only, no target). Pairs with stats.classify_objects. |
| `stats.histogram` | analysis.histogram_threshold and analysis.multiotsu build a histogram to derive a threshold and emit only the threshold/mask. This emits the distribution itself, and adds the per-object scope neither has. |
| `stats.outliers` | `enhance.remove_blobs` (size-based mask cleanup before labelling). This is post-measurement and multivariate. |
| `stats.pca` | none. Requires a multi-attribute selection socket (pick K named attributes), which the current STRING-attribute-name pattern in analysis.reduce_scalar only does one at a time. |
| `table.aggregate` | analysis.reduce_scalar collapses a whole layer or column to one GLOBAL number, with no grouping and one statistic. transform.transfer_domain's coarsen already does the special case 'group by a lattice axis, one reducer'; this generalizes to arbitrary column keys and many statistics at once. |
| `table.bin` | analysis.reduce_scalar reduces a whole domain to one scalar with a fixed reducer set and no predicate. This is the grouped version; it also introduces the categorical-column idea the spreadsheet and overlay colouring both want. |
| `table.computed_column` | analysis.object_metrics writes a fixed menu of derived columns (velocity, neighbours, folds); this writes an arbitrary user expression. object_metrics' frame_fold/self_fold are exactly what a group-aggregate expression would express generically. |
| `table.fill_missing` | none. Depends on core.null_semantics. |
| `table.filter` | analysis.segment's min_area/max_area/min_volume/max_volume cover the size case only for its own output. Adds border/frame removal, radius-based cleaning and top-N, on any incoming mask. |
| `table.find_events` | `detect.spots` / `detect.particles` (spatial detection in the image). Different axis, different backend. |
| `table.manage_columns` | `Dataset.without` (an API method, not a node) |
| `table.normalize_column` | `enhance.normalize` (image intensity, percentile/min-max, with a documented `value_rescaled` meta_transform). Overlap is nominal only — different domain family, different statistics, no bit-depth metadata rewrite. |
| `table.window` | analysis.object_metrics 'self_fold' divides an object's intensity by its own track mean — a single fixed baselining scheme with no filtering, no rolling window, and no events. enhance.temporal_gain filters the IMAGE along t, not a measured column. |
| `track.close_gaps` | none. Could be a Mode on track.stitch, but it changes the member table (adds rows) rather than the membership, which is a different contract. |
| `track.edit` | none. flow.iterate can sweep linker parameters but cannot fix an individual wrong link. |
| `track.flow_prior` | Overlaps registration.stabilize and align.drift in spirit -- but those warp the IMAGE; this leaves pixels alone and only informs the linker's cost. Also overlaps analysis.accumulate_field (which integrates a field over t) -- this node samples that integral at object positions. |
| `track.jump_distance` | Complementary to motion.diffusion_fit: same physics, but fits the displacement DISTRIBUTION rather than its second moment, so it separates populations instead of blending them. |
| `track.lap` | track.link and track.objects exist, and track.objects already has centroid/serialtrack/topology/fingerprint/overlap modes. Scope this as: add the two-step LAP with gap closing plus explicit split/merge topology and per-feature cost penalties. The split/merge lineage may also need a TRACK-domain parent/child relation the domain does not currently carry - check before scoping. |
| `track.lineage` | track.link and track.objects both explicitly collapse merges/splits to 1-1 (nodegraph/tracking.py:175). This restores the capability those two throw away rather than re-implementing the linker. |
| `track.msd` | none. |
| `track.neighbor_exchange` | analysis.voronoi/analysis.tessellate build the adjacency for ONE frame; analysis.object_metrics 'neighbors' gives k-NN distances with no identity across t. Neither can detect an exchange. |
| `track.objects.magik` | track.link and track.objects. Same TRACK contract; distinctive in operating on the detection graph (so it is cheap on CPU), in being torch-free, and in emitting motion-property readouts that no existing node produces. |
| `track.objects.ultrack` | track.link and track.objects both take a fixed segmentation as given. This is the only candidate that treats segmentation and tracking as one joint optimisation, which means it can also be used purely as a segmentation *corrector* for a time series. |
| `track.smooth` | none. analysis.object_metrics differentiates but never smooths; wiring this in front of it is the intended use. |
| `track.stats` | analysis.object_metrics writes PER-TIMEPOINT velocity/speed onto the member layer. Nothing aggregates a whole track. transform.transfer_structure can gather one column to TRACK with one reducer, but path length and straightness are not reductions of an existing column — they are functions of the ordered position sequence. |
| `track.stitch` | Overlaps track.lap's step 2 by design -- it IS that step, exposed as a standalone card so existing linkers benefit. Still worth having even if track.lap ships: track.objects' five linkers cannot use track.lap's step 1. |
| `track.vacf` | none. analysis.object_metrics computes an instantaneous velocity per member but nothing correlates it in time. |
| `transform.advect_points` | analysis.accumulate_field integrates a field into a cumulative field on the lattice; transform.grow_points and transform.rigid move points by a static transform. Neither integrates a time-varying field along a particle path. |
| `transform.image_to_objects` | analysis.label derives ids from connected components and therefore renumbers, splitting any object whose external id spans two components and merging none — the exact opposite of what importing a trusted segmentation needs. The inverse direction (ConvertObjectsToImage) already exists as the raster every LABEL table carries. |
| `transform.rasterize_points` | transform.rasterize_field INTERPOLATES a Point vector/scalar field onto a grid (a smooth reconstruction of an underlying continuous field); transform.grow_points paints each Point as a hard disc/ellipsoid label. This ACCUMULATES a weighted kernel per point -- a density, not an interpolant and not a label -- and supports a per-point sigma, which neither does. |
| `transform.sdf_grid` | enhance.morphology does integer-structuring-element erode/dilate/open/close; analysis.edt gives an unsigned distance transform; analysis.tessellate marching-cubes a label raster. This adds signed distance as a first-class representation, exact-um offsets, curvature, and boolean surface algebra -- none of which the three together can do. |
| `transform.set_position` | transform.rigid moves PIXELS inside a fixed frame (translate + in-plane rotate, cropping and zero-filling); registration.stabilize resamples pixels. Nothing in the catalog moves structure-domain COORDINATES, and nothing applies a non-rigid per-element displacement to anything. |
| `transform.shrink` | This is transform.grow_points with the sign flipped plus two guard rules; the 'shrink to a point' case is already reachable via transform.label_to_points centroid, and the naive erosion case via enhance.morphology, which however erodes the MASK and silently merges or deletes labels. |
| `transform.transfer_structure` | This IS the existing node — it adds the MESH row/column of the bridge matrix, which today has no pairs at all. |
| `transform.warp_field` | transform.rigid is translation/rotation/scale with linear/nearest/cubic interpolation — affine at most. registration.stabilize estimates and applies translation/euclidean/affine/feature models per frame. Neither can apply a per-voxel field, and neither can estimate a non-affine warp from landmarks. transform.rasterize_field converts a sparse field to a dense one, which is the input this node consumes. |
| `util.binning` | util.resample rescales by an arbitrary float factor with interpolation. Binning is exact, integer-factor and statistic-selectable; interpolated downsampling cannot express sum or max reduction and silently changes total intensity. |
| `util.focus_fuse` | util.zproject offers max/mean/sum/min/median — all intensity statistics with no notion of focus, and none emits a height map. |
| `util.join` | channel.merge puts two acquisitions on one CHANNEL axis by stage position; util.stitch merges M into a spatial mosaic; util.stack collapses T. All three operate on the acquisition lattice. None concatenates rows of a structure table. |
| `util.material` | none. The alternative — repeating E/nu/h as params on eight different nodes — is exactly the metadata duplication the derive=/unit= system exists to avoid. |
| `util.phantom` | none. Nothing in the catalog generates data; every fixture with a known answer lives in nodegraph/selftest.py and is unreachable from a graph. |
| `util.plate_map` | util.stitch consumes stage positions to build a mosaic; view.overlay uses absolute stage position for placement. Neither infers a plate grid or attaches condition labels, and no node writes to the MULTIPOINT domain. |
| `util.resize_objects` | util.resample takes scale_xy/scale_z on the image only and interpolates; it has no label-safe order-0 path, no target-size form, and no mean-binning mode. Could ship as two extra modes on util.resample instead (effort S). |
| `util.stage_transform` | view.overlay places one Dataset inside another's field using exactly this maths, and util.stitch uses it for the mosaic — but neither exposes it for structure tables, so a measured object cannot be given a stage coordinate. |
| `util.synthetic_deform` | transform.rigid can apply one affine transform to real data but produces no ground-truth field, no noise model, and no series; there is no synthetic-data source node in the catalog at all. |
| `util.track_stack` | util.crop takes a static box in lattice coordinates. This takes a per-timepoint centre from a track, which crop cannot express, and it multiplies the output by the number of tracks. |
| `util.zproject` | util.zproject — this IS that node with more entries in _Z_METHODS. 'by_reference' needs a second Dataset socket, so it is the only one that is more than a table line. |
| `value.boolean_math` | none. nodegraph/field.py's BinOp can express a mask expression as a FIELD, but no node writes a two-layer expression back as a Voxel layer, and analysis.threshold only produces a mask from an intensity image. |
| `value.dataset_info` | The `derive=` mechanism on sockets consumes metadata to compute a default, but only through built-in named expressions; the user cannot author a new one. This inverts it -- metadata becomes data on a wire. |
| `value.domain_size` | analysis.reduce_scalar's 'count' reducer over a Label column gives the label count as a Global layer -- but as a Dataset-writing node, not a value wire, and only for one domain at a time. |
| `value.field_at_index` | zone.sim_in/sim_out can carry state across t, but as a whole-graph zone: expressing 'this cell's area minus its area last frame' needs a zone plus manual bookkeeping. This is one socket. |
| `value.map_range` | enhance.normalize rescales VOXEL intensities (percentile/minmax) as an image operation. This operates on value/field sockets for parameter wiring and does not touch the image. |
| `value.math` | nodegraph/field.py implements the IR but exposes no node. analysis.threshold applies a comparison and emits only a boolean mask; enhance.normalize applies a fixed rescale. Nothing performs A op B between two layers. |
| `value.named_attribute` | transform.transfer_domain and transform.transfer_structure MOVE a stored layer to another domain and write a new stored layer (a Dataset-in/Dataset-out node, one card per hop). This reads a layer as a lazy expression that transfers implicitly at the point of consumption -- no intermediate layer, no card per hop. |
| `value.sample` | nodegraph.bridges.voxel_to_point already samples at point positions with nearest or linear, and transform.transfer_structure surfaces it as a card. This is the field-native version at an ARBITRARY position field (not only a stored Point table's positions), which is what ray profiles, resampled contours and warped coordinates need. |
| `value.separate_xyz` | none. sockets.can_connect deliberately REFUSES vector narrowing and says it 'needs an explicit drop/swizzle node' -- this is that node. |
| `value.vector_math` | analysis.object_metrics computes speed/divergence/curl for tracked objects as fixed outputs; this makes the vector algebra itself composable on any vector layer, including a DVC field that was never tracked. |
| `view.instance_on_points` | view.overlay composites a layer over the image with blend/display/canvas modes; nodegraph.placement handles placement. Neither generates field-driven glyph geometry -- an arrow whose length and angle come from a vector field. |
| `zone.foreach` | zone.repeat_in/out iterate a fixed number of times; zone.sim_in/out carry state over t; zone.frame runs per-t; flow.iterate sweeps a parameter. None iterates over the ELEMENTS of a domain with each element's own geometry in hand. |

## Appendix D - Rejected proposals (205)

Killed by the fit pass, which had read the engine source. Recorded so the next person does
not re-propose them. A rejection is not always permanent: several are rejected only because
a prerequisite capability is absent, and those reasons say so.

| Proposal | op_key | Survey | Why rejected |
|---|---|---|---|
| Blur Attribute | `value.blur_attribute` | blender-universals | Depends on adjacency that does not exist: MESH_STRATA is only ('vert','face') (mesh.py:75) with no edge bucket, and analysis.voronoi/tessellate emit regions and surfaces but no adjacency layer; the published packing readout is neighbour COUNT (#47), not graph smoothing. |
| Bounding Box / Convex Hull | `analysis.hull_bbox` | blender-universals | analysis.tessellate's convex mode already builds one convex-hull MESH per label with analytic volume and surface area; the residual (an axis-aligned bbox as six um columns) is a min/max away through analysis.reduce_scalar, and grouping by an arbitrary key is #23's Group ID. |
| Bundles and Closures | `core.bundle_closure` | blender-universals | Two new socket kinds plus subgraph-as-value invocation, which engine._entry has no notion of (it walks a fixed DAG of NodeInstances and folds every predecessor's hash into the recipe key), for zero new measurement; premature while there is still no math node and no filter node. |
| Capture Attribute | `value.capture_attribute` | blender-universals | Subsumed by #19 with a generated name — fields here are evaluated at the consuming node against its own Dataset (field.py:172-219), not re-bound to a changed element set, so Blender's re-evaluation hazard does not exist; and the headline 'measure on raw photons before deconvolution' case is already... |
| Clamp | `value.clamp` | blender-universals | min and max are already in field._BINOPS (field.py:123-127) and value.math exposes them; a dedicated clamp adds no capability while costing a full option-doc set (60 chars/option, selftest.py:9980). |
| Color Ramp | `value.color_ramp` | blender-universals | Presentation-only, and needs BOTH a COLOR field branch (field.py has no vector/colour kind) and a new stop-list socket type (SocketType closed, no LIST); view.overlay already owns the display path with four Modes, and readable per-object colouring is #10's hashed-id job. |
| Combine Color / Separate Color | `value.separate_color` | blender-universals | RGB/HSV/HED decomposition serves brightfield histology (H&E/IHC), not live-cell 3D fluorescence ND2 or grayscale DIC; it would need a COLOR field branch in field.py for no in-scope measurement. |
| Dual Mesh | `mesh.dual` | blender-universals | Its only use is epithelial packing, and the adjacency it would produce is exactly what #47 builds directly from centroids; a dual rewrite of the CSR strata is substantial MeshTables.validate invariant risk (mesh.py:163-256) for no measurement not already reachable. |
| Evaluate on Domain | `value.field_on_domain` | blender-universals | For lattice pairs this is field.Attr's existing `reducer` argument (field.py:93) surfaced by #18, which already auto-transfers at field.py:190-207; every structure hop it would add is already a shipped card in transform.transfer_structure (15 pairs, including label->voxel paint-by-label via... |
| Float Curve | `value.float_curve` | blender-universals | Needs a curve-widget socket to serialize control points, but SocketType (sockets.py:24-32) is a closed enum with no LIST/curve member and _CONVERSIONS/SOCKET_COLOR are closed tables; the payoff is a display tone curve, and quantitative response linearization belongs in a calibration node. |
| Float to Integer / Integer Math / Bit Math | `value.integer_math` | blender-universals | add/sub/mul/min/max/abs/sign/power duplicate value.math; the unique additions (gcd/lcm, bit shifts) have no in-scope use, and int-exact id handling is a field-IR dtype-preservation concern rather than a node. |
| Fractional / Coverage Mask (Cryptomatte, ID Mask) | `analysis.fractional_mask` | blender-universals | The per-object soft-matte half is unpredictable at edit time (one layer per object, and extra_layers/propagate_meta are pixel-free by contract, metadata.py:598-630 — the same blocker that kills #34), and the corrected-volume half infers sub-voxel precision from a binarized mask, which is exactly... |
| Gizmos: On-Image Handles Bound to Parameters | `core.gizmos` | blender-universals | registry.PICK_KINDS (registry.py:82-85) already ships 13 gestures covering exactly these cases — 'radius' is literally drag a circle and commit its um radius, 'distance' is a two-click ruler, 'rect'/'zrange' drag bounds via pick_bounds — all type-checked against _PICK_SOCKET_TYPES and memo-neutral;... |
| Hash Value | `value.hash_value` | blender-universals | #10's own stated backend IS a hash of (id, seed), so shipping both duplicates one operator; string keys for grouping arrive with the plate-map import (#60), not from an in-graph hash. |
| Index Switch | `value.index_switch` | blender-universals | N-way selection is #11 chained; it additionally needs variadic named input slots (a NodeSpec's inputs tuple is static — multi=True gives one tuple, engine.py:736-750, not N independently-lazy slots) and the MENU SocketType, which has no producer, no consumer and no entry in _CONVERSIONS anywhere. |
| Is Preview (Viewport vs Final) | `value.is_preview` | blender-universals | A result that depends on an out-of-band evaluation flag either poisons the memo (the flag is not a param, and node_recipe_hash keys only on op_key + params + upstream hashes/revisions + code fingerprint, engine.py:704-718) or makes preview and final two different pipelines — the exact hazard it... |
| Kuwahara | `enhance.kuwahara` | blender-universals | A fifth edge-preserving smoother beside bilateral/nlm/tv_denoise/median — distinct in mechanism but it unlocks no downstream graph, and the anisotropic-fibre case is better served by a structure-tensor orientation node that produces a measurement rather than a prettier image. |
| Mix | `value.mix` | blender-universals | The float/vector half is a + (b-a)*f, already expressible with existing field._BINOPS through value.math; the remainder (hue/saturation/value/overlay/screen blend modes) is photo compositing and needs a COLOR field branch for no measurement payoff. |
| Node Tools / Operators | `core.node_tools` | blender-universals | Needs a selection/active-element context and per-element mutation, but there is no mutate-in-place path anywhere — a node re-emits the whole StructureTable via with_structure (dataset.py:260) and AttributeLayer.values is frozen read-only (dataset.py:146-168); segmentation proofreading is an editor... |
| Parameter Drivers, Expressions and Time Keyframes | `core.param_drivers` | blender-universals | The surveyor concedes fields subsume the data half; an expression box plus keyframe curve would be a THIRD parallel parameter mechanism beside derive= (already evaluated at two sites, metadata.eval_derive:732 and ChannelContext.param, engine.py:501-515) and the field IR — and a param varying over t... |
| Set ID | `value.set_id` | blender-universals | The column half is already the shipped track->label broadcast pair in transform.transfer_structure (bridges.broadcast_track); the remaining half rewrites `id`, the invariant primary key that TrackMembership rows and the mesh element foreign keys reference (structure.py:39, mesh.py:210-243), so it... |
| Split to Instances / Duplicate Elements | `analysis.split_to_groups` | blender-universals | The group count is data-dependent, but NodeSpec.extra_layers and propagate_meta run on every keystroke and are pixel-free by design (metadata.py:598-630) — so N per-group layer instances cannot be predicted for the edit-time layer catalog; grouped statistics are already covered by #23's Group ID... |
| Active Contour | `analysis.active_contour` | cellprofiler-skimage | Iterative full-volume evolution per (m,t) on a CPU-only box buys smoother outlines for shape metrics that analysis.measure already restricts to the 2D-only three; the cost/benefit does not survive a 49-position 3D series. |
| Assay Statistics | `analysis.assay_stats` | cellprofiler-skimage | Plate-screening statistics for a mechanobiology lab, gated behind a grouping facility and a plot artifact, on control/dose metadata this single-file workflow does not carry. |
| Calibrate Denoiser | `enhance.calibrate_denoiser` | cellprofiler-skimage | Duplicates five existing nodes' surfaces inside one compute to choose a parameter that flow.iterate already exists to sweep, and it collapses per-node memo granularity for a strength choice nobody publishes. |
| Composite (Gray to Color) | `view.composite` | cellprofiler-skimage | Figure rendering with burned-in furniture is a viewer/export concern, not a measurement, and the layer shape it asks for does not exist. |
| Flood Select | `analysis.flood_select` | cellprofiler-skimage | A single picked seed does not survive a pull over 49 positions x T x Z — there is no per-(m,t) seed table, so the node is an interactive one-off that cannot compose. |
| Hough Shapes | `analysis.hough` | cellprofiler-skimage | 2D-only, and both halves are covered: bead/droplet detection already has a validated subpixel path in kernels/bead_detect.py, and fiber orientation comes out of candidate 42's Hessian eigenvectors. |
| Hu and Inertia Moments | `analysis.moments` | cellprofiler-skimage | Folds entirely into candidate 20's _MEASURE_SHAPE vocab (inertia_tensor_eigvals is one more regionprops property) and the Hu half is 2D-only with no consumer; a separate op_key adds no capability. |
| Line Profile | `analysis.profile_line` | cellprofiler-skimage | Its deliverable is a figure and the engine has no plot artifact (a compute must return a Dataset, memo.py:145); as data it would be arc-length samples nothing downstream consumes. |
| Local Binary Pattern | `analysis.lbp` | cellprofiler-skimage | Subsumed by candidate 24 as its second family mode by the survey's own admission, and 24 is itself rejected here. |
| Mask Objects | `analysis.mask_objects` | cellprofiler-skimage | Ships as the `mask` mode of analysis.filter_objects with one extra trim rule; a separate op_key duplicates that node's whole socket surface for no new capability. |
| Match Histogram | `enhance.match_histogram` | cellprofiler-skimage | A non-monotone remap destroys the intensity quantitation every granule measurement here depends on, and the multipoint-comparability case it targets is properly solved by candidate 46's physical illumination model. |
| Polar Unwrap | `transform.polar` | cellprofiler-skimage | The per-object form is inexpressible (it would need one lattice per label) and the whole-frame form answers the same question as candidate 23 while breaking the single-pixel-size calibration model every downstream unit conversion assumes. |
| Reduce Object Features | `analysis.dimreduce` | cellprofiler-skimage | Premature — its input (dozens of per-object feature columns) does not exist since candidates 21, 24 and 25 are rejected — and its output duplicates analysis.cluster_points' cluster column. |
| Superpixels | `analysis.superpixels` | cellprofiler-skimage | Over-segmentation front-half for texture workflows this lab does not run; the fluorescence targets are blob-like and the transmitted-light route is better served by candidates 62/8. |
| Surface Curvature | `analysis.mesh_curvature` | cellprofiler-skimage | Per-vertex curvature could not reach any consumer — Mesh is completely isolated in the transfer graph — and the droplet-stress workflow it serves is not this lab's. |
| Texture (Haralick) | `analysis.texture` | cellprofiler-skimage | GLCM at four offsets per object per plane is not affordable on a 49-position 3D timelapse (the survey concedes this in its own LBP entry), it is 2D-only, and no classifier consumes the block; candidate 26 is the affordable pattern measurement for this data. |
| Tone Curve | `enhance.gamma` | cellprofiler-skimage | Display tone-mapping: equalize_hist duplicates enhance.clahe's local form and log/sigmoid destroy the photometric linearity the app exists to preserve, with no measurement downstream. |
| Unwrap Phase | `analysis.unwrap_phase` | cellprofiler-skimage | It has no producer: no node in the catalog emits a wrapped-phase layer, and analysis.dvc_field's phase mode consumes phase internally and never exposes it. |
| Zernike Moments | `analysis.zernike` | cellprofiler-skimage | 2D-only descriptor whose only consumer is the feature-space classifier that also does not exist, with no granule, organelle or deformation use in this lab. |
| Accumulate Field | `table.accumulate` | data-stats-io | Rejected: cumulative sum/product/min/max are already inside cand 17's declared scope, so this is one node's worth of duplication plus a label collision with the existing analysis.accumulate_field (DVC). |
| Append Columns | `table.append_columns` | data-stats-io | Rejected: measuring the same objects twice is already done by chaining measure and object_metrics on one Dataset, and the separate-branch case is table.join keyed on the invariant `id` column — no new capability. |
| Dose Response / EC50 | `stats.dose_response` | data-stats-io | Rejected: one more model plus a bootstrap CI on cand 36, and compound dose-response is not this lab's stated assay. |
| Gaussian Mixture Decomposition (1-D) | `stats.mixture_decompose` | data-stats-io | Rejected: it is cand 46's GMM mode with one feature column, and the component-curve output is cand 36's already-declared sum-of-Gaussians model. |
| Hit Calling | `screen.hit_call` | data-stats-io | Rejected: thresholding a normalized score into a flag is cand 13 + cand 0 with a comparison operator; the SSMD bands and hit-rate warning are documentation, not machinery, and this lab does not run hit lists. |
| Kernel Density Estimate | `stats.kde` | data-stats-io | Rejected: a one-call smoother whose only real consumer is a violin render that can compute it inline, and as a standalone node it emits a curve table nothing in the catalog reads. |
| Multi-Input Dataset Socket | `core.multi_input_dataset` | data-stats-io | Rejected: the premise is factually wrong — N-ary Dataset inputs are already expressible and shipping; what remains (user-draggable link order) is a nodelab_v2 nicety, and the real limit (propagate_meta reads only dpreds[0]) is not what this card proposes to fix. |
| Nonlinear Embedding (UMAP / t-SNE) | `stats.embedding` | data-stats-io | Rejected: purely a visualization aid that, with no figure artifact, writes coordinate columns nothing can plot, and cand 44 already covers the interpretable dimensionality reduction this lab needs. |
| Pivot / Cross Table | `table.pivot` | data-stats-io | Rejected: a pure long/wide reshape with no measurement content, whose only consumer (a heatmap render) can pivot inline, and whose matrix output has nowhere legal to live. |
| Plate Normalization | `screen.plate_normalize` | data-stats-io | Rejected: B-score and percent-of-control presuppose designated control wells on a screening plate, which is not this lab's assay; the realistic need (per-group robust centring) is cand 21's declared scope. |
| Plot: Fit / Residual | `plot.fit` | data-stats-io | Rejected as a separate card: a fit overlay plus a residual panel is an option set on cand 53/54 consuming cand 36/38/51 output, and splitting it out buys only a naming convention. |
| Plot: Histogram / Distribution | `plot.histogram` | data-stats-io | Rejected as a separate card: once cand 33 emits the bin table, drawing it is exactly cand 55's bar/step mode or cand 54's line mode — keeping a plot node per chart type is the fusion habit this rebuild is trying to escape. |
| Plot: Lineage Tree | `plot.lineage_tree` | data-stats-io | Rejected as scoped: the card admits it implies a lineage model extension, and that branching decision belongs to nodegraph/tracking.py and track.link/track.objects as its own piece of work, not to a plot node. |
| Plot: Matrix Heatmap | `plot.heatmap` | data-stats-io | Rejected: its input does not exist in a storable form, and for a granule/DVC lab the feature-by-condition clustered heatmap is a profiling-screen presentation well behind the scatter/line/categorical trio in value. |
| Plot: Parallel Coordinates | `plot.parallel_coords` | data-stats-io | Rejected: the brushing half needs GUI interaction machinery that does not exist, and without it the analytic content is covered by cand 44/53. |
| Plot: Plate Heatmap | `plot.plate_heatmap` | data-stats-io | Rejected: plate-geometry glyph rendering is a screening-vendor presentation feature for a lab whose benchmark is a single well, and the artefact it would reveal is already detected numerically by cand 30. |
| Plot: Rose / Angular Histogram | `plot.rose` | data-stats-io | Rejected as a separate card: it is a polar axis Mode on cand 52/55, and cand 50 already produces the circular mean, R and Rayleigh p that carry the actual claim. |
| Plot: Track / Trajectory | `plot.track_plot` | data-stats-io | Rejected: once tracks are re-origined by a vector-difference column, drawing them is cand 53/54, so the distinct content reduces to a LineCollection style choice. |
| Post-hoc Comparisons | `stats.posthoc` | data-stats-io | Rejected: both stock estimators live in the same scipy.stats namespace cand 39 already imports, so this is a mode set on that node, not a separate card, and the compact-letter display is a figure-annotation detail. |
| Rank / Percentile | `table.rank` | data-stats-io | Rejected: a node per numpy/scipy function is the anti-composability failure this repo is trying to escape — rank belongs as an operation available to cand 13's computed column, not as its own card. |
| Read OME-Zarr | `io.read_ome_zarr` | data-stats-io | Rejected for now: the lab's data is ND2 and io.load covers it, coarse-level prototyping is what io.dock already provides, and the collaborator-data case is better served by converting externally than by standing up a second source-node pathway. |
| Read via Bio-Formats | `io.read_bioformats` | data-stats-io | Rejected: a Java runtime is a disproportionate dependency for an ND2-centric lab, and the card itself concedes that JVM-free per-format plugins are the better first step. |
| Rename / Reorder / Format Columns | `table.rename_columns` | data-stats-io | Rejected: pure export/display cosmetics with no measurement content, and renaming layer keys invites exactly the LayerKey-slot confusion the data model documents against. |
| Sort Rows | `table.sort` | data-stats-io | Rejected: element order is load-bearing for nothing in a pull-based graph (cumulative/window ops can sort internally), the spreadsheet already sorts for display, and renumbering ids to reorder risks raster/membership divergence for no analytical gain. |
| Write HDF5 | `io.write_hdf5` | data-stats-io | Rejected: it overlaps io.dock (private checkpoint), cand 65 (image interchange) and cand 72 (tables) simultaneously — 'one file carrying pixels and tables' is a packaging preference, not a measurement capability. |
| 3D RCAN Denoise & Sharpen | `enhance.rcan3d` | deep-learning | Duplicates enhance.care/care_isotropic with a heavier unmaintained TF1-era research repo, needs a paired high-resolution target this lab cannot acquire on live cells, and 3D attention blocks fight the 64 GiB CPU allocator cap. |
| Deep SMLM Localization (DeepSTORM / DECODE) | `detect.localize_smlm` | deep-learning | Presumes an SMLM acquisition (thousands of blinking frames of a static field), which is a different experiment from this lab's live-cell 3D multipoint ND2 — the survey flags it low-priority itself. |
| Diversity Denoising with Uncertainty (DivNoising / HDN) | `enhance.divnoising` | deep-learning | No sample axis exists for the N-draws output that is the node's whole point, and CPU-only VAE training plus a required noise model puts it furthest out of budget in the survey. |
| EmbedTrack / Joint Segment-and-Track | `track.embedtrack` | deep-learning | Same joint-segment-and-track idea as ultrack at strictly higher setup cost, 2D only, and it needs training or a CTC checkpoint the lab does not have — the survey's own priority ordering agrees. |
| Fine-Tune Model | `learn.finetune` | deep-learning | Fine-tuning is per-architecture, not generic; it belongs as a `train`/`warm_start` Mode inside each segmenter node (stardist, cellpose) where the checkpoint format and loss already live, not as a standalone learn.finetune. |
| Fluctuation Super-Resolution (SRRF / eSRRF) | `enhance.srrf` | deep-learning | Requires a high-frame-rate burst of a static field, which a 49-position revisit schedule does not produce; and per-burst grouping is a new axis concept, not a node. |
| Fourier Channel Attention Super-Resolution (DFCAN/DFGAN) | `enhance.dfcan` | deep-learning | Pretrained weights are structure-specific to BioSR classes (microtubules/CCP/F-actin/ER), none of which are this lab's granules, and no SIM ground truth exists here — a hallucinating SR prior upstream of a granule count is a measurement liability, not a feature. |
| Learned Deformable Registration (VoxelMorph) | `registration.voxelmorph` | deep-learning | Pretrained VoxelMorph weights are brain-MRI, CPU 3D training inside the 64 GiB allocator cap is out of budget, and candidate 40 already delivers the deformable field and the DVC cross-check without a training step. |
| Mask Propagation (SAM 2 Video) | `learn.propagate_masks` | deep-learning | Video memory attention on CPU across a 49-position multi-hundred-timepoint series is out of budget by orders of magnitude, and ultrack (candidate 44) delivers temporal-evidence segmentation repair torch-free. |
| Model cache & weights manager | `core.model_cache` | deep-learning | Not a graph capability: the load-once singleton (already implemented in kernels/stardist_segment.py) and hash-pinning belong in the shared model loader candidates 17/18 already require, and browsing a zoo is a browser's job. |
| Pixel Classifier → Pretrained-Feature Backend | `analysis.pixel_classify` | deep-learning | Adds torch + torchvision for a second feature backend on a node that does not exist yet; premature, and the µm-scaled classical filter bank must be shown to fail on this lab's data first. |
| Segmentation → Mesmer Method | `analysis.segment` | deep-learning | The 'no new runtime' premise is false: deepcell-tf pins an old TensorFlow against the 2.21 installed here, and the weights are still behind a DeepCell API key — a hard dependency conflict on top of the token gate. |
| Segmentation → Omnipose Method | `analysis.segment` | deep-learning | Omnipose ships as a shadowing cellpose fork (cellpose-omni) that cannot co-exist with the plain cellpose install candidate 0 requires; the filamentous-morphology delta does not justify a mutually-exclusive second torch segmenter. |
| Segmentation → SplineDist Method | `analysis.segment` | deep-learning | An unmaintained TF1-era stardist fork that will not load under TF 2.21 / Keras 3 (this repo has already paid for legacy-.h5 layer-order hazards); the concavity fix arrives on a maintained stack via candidates 0 and 2. |
| Segmentation-Aware Restoration (Cellpose3) | `enhance.cellpose_restore` | deep-learning | Its loss optimises segmentability, not photometry, so it is forbidden upstream of this lab's granule-intensity measurement — and the one unique piece (upsample) is served by util.resample plus a correctly µm-derived diameter on the cellpose method. |
| Self-Supervised Deconvolution (DecoNoising) | `enhance.deconv_selfsup` | deep-learning | Either adds torch or requires reimplementing a research network on TensorFlow, for a third point on a trade-off curve already bracketed by enhance.deconvolve and enhance.zs_deconvnet. |
| Semantic Classes (nnU-Net) | `learn.semantic` | deep-learning | There is no pretrained nnU-Net configuration for this lab's fluorescence data, and CPU training of a 3d_fullres config is out of budget — the reachable semantic models are all covered by candidate 13's generic path. |
| Temporal Noise2Noise | `enhance.noise2noise` | deep-learning | The two-independent-realisations-of-the-same-signal assumption is violated precisely by the motile cells this lab images; a motion gate that rejects most pairs leaves too little training signal to justify a separate node. |
| Track Objects → Trackastra Method | `track.objects` | deep-learning | Torch plus a documented Windows/Python-3.10 constraint against this 3.12/3.13 environment (pycache verified), for the second learned linker behind ultrack — which is torch-free and strictly more capable. |
| Train Model | `learn.train` | deep-learning | As specified it is a god node; the real deliverable is lifting zs_deconvnet's checkpoint + settings-hash machinery into `catalog/_shared/`, after which each task ships as its own node (candidates 23, 25, 26) — proposing the mega-node instead of the helper is the mistake. |
| Train zone | `core.zone_train` | deep-learning | Architecturally impossible as specified: a training loop of thousands of steps cannot be an unrolled zone, and persistent optimiser state across steps is exactly what unrolling cannot carry; a patch-sampler node feeding a trainer gives the inspectable-patches benefit without the zone. |
| Chamfer / Anisotropic Weights on Distance Transform | `analysis.edt` | fiji-imagej | Its stated premise is false — anisotropic calibrated sampling is already implemented — and chamfer/chessboard metrics are strictly worse approximations of a transform that is already exact; only `signed` remains, which is one BinOp. |
| Degree of Anisotropy (MIL) | `analysis.anisotropy_mil` | fiji-imagej | Bone-stereology measure whose actionable output (a single anisotropy scalar and a director) is already delivered by candidates 24+26 on the data this lab actually images, and it demands a clean binary volume these stacks rarely give. |
| Directional Filter | `enhance.directional` | fiji-imagej | Its downstream job (survive-the-opening for elongated structure) is served better and far cheaper by candidate 27's Hessian vesselness, and its orientation output is a worse estimate than candidate 24's structure tensor. |
| Directionality Histogram | `analysis.directionality` | fiji-imagej | Its actionable numbers (preferred direction, dispersion, goodness) are the nematic eigen-decomposition of candidate 26 applied to candidate 24's field, so it is a second implementation of an answer the graph would already have. |
| Ellipsoid Factor | `analysis.ellipsoid_factor` | fiji-imagej | XL effort for a rod-vs-plate descriptor with no published Python reference output to validate against, which project memory says is the one thing that makes a vendored numerical port trustworthy. |
| Feature-Based Registration | `registration.features` | fiji-imagej | Already shipped — the feature/ORB+RANSAC path, the higher-order models, the template reference and the confidence report all exist; only a projective model would be new. |
| Fractal Dimension & Lacunarity | `analysis.fractal_dimension` | fiji-imagej | Box-counting D on a thresholded confocal plane is dominated by the threshold and the scale range rather than the biology, and neither number appears in this lab's stated readouts. |
| Gabor Filter Bank | `enhance.gabor` | fiji-imagej | A texture feature bank whose only consumer is the XL, brush-blocked classifier (candidate 50); its orientation/scale output duplicates candidates 24 and 28 for everything this lab reports. |
| Geodesic Shape Metrics | `analysis.geodesic_metrics` | fiji-imagej | Niche for a granule/monolayer/gel lab: measure.py already emits 3D-valid axis_major/axis_minor/solidity/extent with µm spacing, and the honest-width half is covered by candidate 19's per-voxel local thickness. |
| Kuwahara Filter | `enhance.kuwahara` | fiji-imagej | Five edge-preserving denoisers already ship (bilateral, nlm, median, tv_denoise, wavelet_denoise); interior flattening is a marginal delta that candidate 3's dynamic parameter addresses more directly at the actual failure point. |
| Matrix Pattern Summary | `analysis.matrix_pattern` | fiji-imagej | A fixed battery over four nodes that do not exist yet; ship it as a shipped example graph once its components land, and its independent value (published reference numbers) is a test fixture, not a node. |
| Montage & Figure Annotation | `view.montage` | fiji-imagej | Figure layout with burned-in text is presentation, not measurement, and view/overlay.py plus nodelab_v2/export.py already own the annotate-and-bake path. |
| Polar Transform | `transform.polar` | fiji-imagej | Cortical-perimeter periodicity is a niche readout here, and the biology it serves is already reachable through candidates 16 and 33 without paying for an axis-rewriting node. |
| Reslice | `transform.reslice` | fiji-imagej | Orthogonal views are a viewer concern (nodelab_v2/glview.py exists) rather than a measurement, and for measurement purposes util.resample plus a z-aware measure covers the calibrated cases at far lower risk. |
| Ridge Detection | `detect.ridges` | fiji-imagej | L-effort hand port of a Java reference with no validated Python equivalent, producing output the domain graph cannot measure; filament tracing is also not a stated readout for this lab. |
| Spatial Relations (RCC8) | `analysis.spatial_relations` | fiji-imagej | The two decisions that actually matter (contained-in vs merely-overlapping) are already available as candidate 15's overlap-fraction and centroid-inside tests; the full RCC8D taxonomy adds vocabulary, not measurement. |
| Straighten | `transform.straighten` | fiji-imagej | Rectifying a curved structure serves neurite/gut/vessel work, not granules in cells, monolayers or gels, and it competes with candidate 30 for the same L-effort sampler. |
| Structuring Element Shape | `enhance.morphology` | fiji-imagej | The anisotropy justification is already covered by the node's radius_z socket plus the dim lever's plane-vs-volume split; the residual disk/square/octagon/diamond menu is cosmetic and the line SE belongs to candidate 8, which is itself rejected. |
| Temporal Color Code | `view.temporal_color` | fiji-imagej | A figure-production convenience rather than a measurement, and the QC signal it provides is available from the existing viewer plus overlay compositing. |
| Autofluorescence Removal | `channel.autofluorescence` | imaris-commercial | A mode of channel.unmix by the surveyor's own admission, and they explicitly did not verify the algorithm behind the cited paper — too thin a spec to build an L-effort numerical node from. |
| Bleed-Through Correction | `channel.crosstalk` | imaris-commercial | The surveyor themselves say it is best shipped as a MODE of channel.unmix (square, identity-dominant matrix estimated from the data); two op_keys for one linear-inverse compute would duplicate the whole cross-c provider. |
| Build Coloc Channel | `coloc.channel` | imaris-commercial | The surveyor's own note concedes it is a preset of value.math; a two-input channel-arithmetic special case should not be its own op_key when candidate 49 is the stated composability goal. |
| Clip / Section Surface | `mesh.clip` | imaris-commercial | Cross-sectional area along an object's own axis is what candidate 47 (split_objects) already gives on the label raster, and oblique sectioning for figures is a viewer concern (candidate 60), not a measurement node. |
| Costes Threshold & Significance | `coloc.costes` | imaris-commercial | Auto-threshold selection and a randomization P-value are the defensibility half of coloc.pixel, not a separate object — ship them as a `threshold: manual/costes` mode plus a `significance_iters` param on candidate 19 rather than a second L-effort node. |
| Filament Tracing | `filament.trace` | imaris-commercial | XL cost-field tracer squarely aimed at neurites and vessels — not this lab's remit — and it depends on the rejected GRAPH domain. |
| Fuse Overlapping Volumes | `util.fuse_views` | imaris-commercial | It extends util.stitch's existing M→1 mosaic with a blend mode and a z-overlap rule rather than adding a distinct capability; those belong as modes on stitch, where the MULTI_VIEW provider and the origin_um double-count fence already live. |
| Geodesic Distance On Surface | `mesh.geodesic` | imaris-commercial | Membrane-diffusion/neurite tool with no consumer in this lab's remit, and its stated convolutedness readout (geodesic:Euclidean ratio) duplicates mesh.curvature's bending-energy summary. |
| Object-Based Colocalization | `relate.colocalize_objects` | imaris-commercial | It is a composition of relate.overlap (fractions), relate.nearest (within-distance) and candidate 40's randomization envelope — three nodes already proposed — so it adds a bundled preset rather than a capability. |
| PSF From Beads | `enhance.psf_distill` | imaris-commercial | Presupposes a bead-calibration acquisition workflow that this plate-based live-cell pipeline does not have, and the consumer socket it exists to feed does not exist either — two changes deep for a QC readout. |
| Points Inside Surface | `mesh.enclosed_points` | imaris-commercial | A strict subset of candidate 14 (relate.contain), which lists POINT-in-MESH and LABEL-in-MESH among its containment rules — shipping both would be two definitions of the same relation. |
| Remesh Surface | `mesh.remesh` | imaris-commercial | Coarsening duplicates mesh.decimate and refinement duplicates mesh.smooth's conditioning role; the only unique part (hole fill / manifold repair so enclosed_volume_um3 is meaningful) belongs as a mode on those two, not a third mesh-rewriting node. |
| Sample Along Surface Normal | `mesh.ray_sample` | imaris-commercial | The biology it targets (cortex intensity profile, shell thickness) is delivered more robustly and far more cheaply by candidate 39's signed-EDT shells on the label raster; ray casting per vertex adds fragility, not capability. |
| Sholl / Radial Profile | `filament.sholl` | imaris-commercial | The one in-remit use (granule density vs distance from the nucleus) is exactly candidate 39's shell profile done properly with signed EDT; the rest is neuro arborization, out of remit. |
| Skeleton / Graph structure domain | `core.domain_graph` | imaris-commercial | An XL new domain whose only consumers are the filament family (vessels, neurites, spines) — outside this lab's stated remit of granule/organelle quantification and DIC/DVC mechanobiology. |
| Skeleton Network Statistics | `filament.graph` | imaris-commercial | Depends on the rejected GRAPH domain to be honest, and vascular/neurite branch statistics are outside this lab's remit; mitochondrial-network fragmentation alone does not justify an L node plus a new dep. |
| Skeletonize | `filament.skeletonize` | imaris-commercial | With core.domain_graph rejected the output is a bare skeleton mask plus a radius layer that nothing downstream can measure, and the network readouts it exists to feed (candidate 28) are out of remit. |
| Spine / Protrusion Detection | `filament.spines` | imaris-commercial | XL, sits on top of two rejected capabilities, and dendritic-spine morphometry is not this lab's measurement. |
| Surface Boolean | `mesh.boolean` | imaris-commercial | VTK's boolean filter is notoriously fragile on marching-cubes/alpha-shape surfaces (non-manifold, self-intersecting) so the L-effort node would fail silently on real data, and the measurement it exists to deliver (overlapped volume, fraction of each operand) is served robustly by relate.overlap on... |
| Surface Normals & Orientation | `mesh.normals` | imaris-commercial | Winding repair is a correctness fix that belongs inside mesh.py's producers/validate (where faces_are_closed already lives), not a user-facing node, and the orientation-tensor half duplicates analysis.measure's inertia axis_major/axis_minor family. |
| Bayesian Inversion Stress Microscopy | `mech.bism` | mechanobio | A second inference framework for the identical quantity as mech.monolayer_stress, which itself does not exist yet; the posterior error bars are a refinement, not a capability gap. |
| Contractile Moment And Force Dipole | `mech.contractile_moment` | mechanobio | Subsumed by candidate 29, which already lists the contractile-moment trace among its per-object columns; splitting the moment tensor into its own node multiplies the traction-aggregation surface without adding a capability. |
| Deform Mesh By Field | `transform.deform_mesh` | mechanobio | The single-step case is exactly transform.warp_field's target-domain Mode and the multi-step case is analysis.accumulate_field's existing advection with its input restriction relaxed — a third node here would be the same integration written a third time. |
| Deformable Sensor Stress | `mech.sensor_stress` | mechanobio | Requires calibrated embedded force sensors (oil microdroplets / elastic microgels) that this workflow does not use, so there is no data on which to exercise or validate it. |
| Deformation Microscopy (Hyperelastic Warping) | `mech.deformation_microscopy` | mechanobio | XL with no reference implementation or golden output to score against — precisely the configuration this lab's own history says produces a self-consistently wrong port — and ~80% of the readout is reachable by composing optical flow + smoothing + the field operator. |
| FEM Mesh From Label | `transform.fem_mesh` | mechanobio | Duplicates a shipped node for the surface case; the only genuine gap is tetrahedral meshing, which needs pygalmesh/tetgen (no clean Windows wheel) and serves nothing but the unbuilt mech.traction_3d. |
| Field QC Summary | `mech.field_qc` | mechanobio | Pure aggregation of columns that 7/13 already write; it duplicates analysis.reduce_scalar plus a threshold and would harden a per-frame 'verdict' policy into a node before the fields it judges even exist. |
| Field Regression (Effective Rheology) | `mech.field_regression` | mechanobio | Self-described as 'a synthesis, not a port of one paper' — the constitutive-coefficient framing is too vague to specify, and the defensible core (a masked two-field regression) belongs as a mode on the existing reduce node. |
| Helmholtz Decomposition | `mech.helmholtz` | mechanobio | A monolayer-flavoured refinement of div/curl, which analysis.object_field already reports and candidate 39 would give unit-correctly for any field; the energy-fraction summary does not justify a Poisson solver at this stage. |
| Junction Tension Inference | `mech.force_inference` | mechanobio | Vertex-model force inference targets epithelial morphogenesis, not live-cell 3D granule or DVC work, and it needs a domain for cell-pair quantities that does not exist — highest effort-to-relevance ratio in the survey. |
| Line Tension Across Cell Boundaries | `mech.line_tension` | mechanobio | Sits downstream of an unbuilt stress field AND needs a per-junction output the domain model cannot express; the geometry half it would integrate over (extract_boundary, boundary_band) already ships and can wait. |
| Reference-Free Traction (Marker Lattice) | `mech.traction_reference_free` | mechanobio | Requires printed quantum-dot/micropattern marker-lattice substrates that this workflow does not use, so it can be neither exercised nor validated on any data the lab has; L effort for an unusable capability. |
| Temporal Correlation And Persistence | `analysis.temporal_correlation` | mechanobio | Both halves are already covered: per-track persistence is an explicit output of candidate 42, and the field half is candidate 40's FFT autocorrelation with t as the axis — make it an axis Mode there rather than a third correlation node. |
| Topological Defects | `analysis.topological_defects` | mechanobio | An active-matter monolayer readout with no bearing on granule/organelle quantification or DVC mechanobiology; ship analysis.orientation_field first and revisit only if the lab starts running confluent-sheet experiments. |

| Burn-In / Annotate | `view.burn_in` | nikon-ga3 | A figure/movie renderer is the GUI layer's concern, not a DATASET-wire node: hard limit #6 forecloses non-image artifacts, an RGB composite needs a c-axis expansion because layers are scalar, and the viewer already composites LUTs, outlines and overlays. |
| Change Bit Depth | `util.bitdepth` | nikon-ga3 | No consumer: there is no file-output node in the catalog (hard limit #6 — only `nodegraph/checkpoint.py` writes, driven by the GUI's `io.dock`), `_map_image` works in float64 throughout, and the bit_depth metadata machinery (`bit_depth_after_sum`, `value_rescaled`) already exists for nodes to... |

| Convex Hull | `mask.convex_hull` | nikon-ga3 | `analysis.measure` already exposes solidity/extent for the scalar question, and a hull raster plus a speculative concavity mask feeds no measurement this lab has named — high per-label regionprops-slice cost for a display artefact. |

| Elliptic Fourier Shape Descriptors | `analysis.shape_fourier` | readouts | 2D-contour-only descriptor with a single speculative consumer (40); measure's shape metrics plus GLCM already cover shape scoring for blobby organelles. |
| FLIM Phasor | `analysis.flim_phasor` | readouts | ND2 carries no TCSPC and no FLIM instrument is in scope; unbuildable regardless of node design. |
| FLIP Efflux Fit | `analysis.flip` | readouts | Collapses into frap+curve_fit with a different reference region; a separate node buys nothing and the assay is not in evidence here. |
| Gabor Filter Bank | `analysis.gabor_bank` | readouts | Redundant with structure_tensor (10) + MIL (50) for the fibre-alignment question this lab actually asks, and its natural output is a scale×orientation stack with nowhere to live. |
| Joint Intensity Histogram | `analysis.joint_histogram` | readouts | The non-interactive half is just a threshold pair already expressible with layer_math+threshold; the useful half is a plot-panel feature (61), not a node. |
| Lifetime / Decay Axis | `core.lifetime_axis` | readouts | ND2 carries no TCSPC and no FLIM instrument is in scope; correctly flagged as out of scope, so record the decision and stop. |
| Linear Unmixing | `analysis.unmix` | readouts | General n-component unmixing needs lambda stacks and a calibration payload; both are absent, and the practical 2-4 channel case is node 34. |
| Local Binary Pattern | `analysis.lbp` | readouts | Duplicates the texture slot GLCM (6) fills, is 2D-only on 3D data, and its real output is a per-object histogram that needs the PROFILE domain. |
| Noise-Corrected Colocalization | `analysis.coloc_rbncc` | readouts | Requires a replicate-exposure acquisition protocol with no evidence this lab runs it, and otherwise only divides node 0's output by two more Pearsons. |
| Protrusion / Retraction Map | `analysis.protrusion_map` | readouts | Single-assay, single-consumer node with the survey's worst output-shape mismatch; edge morphodynamics is adjacent to, not on, the stated program. |
| Ratiometric FRET | `analysis.fret_ratio` | readouts | Once layer_math (20) exists this is convention, not capability, and no FRET acquisition is in evidence for this lab. |
| Ratiometric Ion Imaging | `analysis.ion_ratio` | readouts | Thin assay wrapper over node 20 for a dye class this lab is not documented to use. |
| Sensitized-Emission FRET | `analysis.fret_se` | readouts | Three-cube sensitized-emission FRET with G/k calibration is far off this lab's axis and the most expensive node in the family. |
| Sholl Analysis | `analysis.sholl` | readouts | Neuron/microglia morphometric with no counterpart in this lab's samples, and structurally a mode of radial_profile. |
| Spectral Phasor | `analysis.spectral_phasor` | readouts | Requires spectral-detector lambda stacks this plate-scanning workflow does not acquire, and its scatter output needs the plot panel to be usable. |
| Zernike Moments | `analysis.zernike` | readouts | Rotation-invariant moments add nothing measure+GLCM do not for blobby granules, and pull in mahotas for a descriptor no kept consumer requires. |
| Bayesian Tracker (btrack) | `track.bayesian` | tracking-lineage | Redundant with track.lap for this lab's assays while costing an XL compiled dependency, a MILP solver and the full lineage model — keep one global tracker and keep the dep-free one. |
| Categorical / label-valued attribute columns | `core.categorical_column` | tracking-lineage | An int code column plus a `choices` socket already carries the category; the residual value is GUI polish, and the field-IR half is moot while nothing in the catalog can produce a Field. |
| Cell Cycle Phase | `analysis.cell_cycle_phase` | tracking-lineage | Requires a FUCCI reporter this lab does not run — the machinery serves exactly one assay and unlocks nothing else in the graph. |
| Chemotaxis Metrics | `motion.chemotaxis` | tracking-lineage | Accumulated/Euclidean distance, directionality and velocity duplicate track.features; the FMI remainder needs a gradient axis only a chemotaxis assay defines, which is not one of this lab's assays. |
| Conservation Tracking | `track.conservation` | tracking-lineage | Same unvalidatable from-scratch XL problem as track.viterbi, and it additionally needs a trained object-count classifier that has no home in this app. |
| Direction Autocorrelation | `motion.direction_autocorr` | tracking-lineage | Duplicates motion.vacf's node shape and code path with a different inner product — a Mode, not a card. |
| Global Viterbi Track Linking | `track.viterbi` | tracking-lineage | A from-scratch XL reimplementation of a 2015 MATLAB tracker with no reference implementation or golden output to score against — project rule is that a green selftest is not validation. |
| ILP Tracking with Segmentation Hypotheses (Ultrack) | `track.ilp` | tracking-lineage | XL with a solver and an on-disk candidate database whose state the memo cannot see, on a box with no commercial solver — not deliverable or auditable here. |
| LINK (edge) domain | `core.link_domain` | tracking-lineage | While each member has exactly one successor the per-link quantity already lives on the member row (object_metrics' velocity); it only becomes necessary after divisions, an assay outside the focus. |
| Learned Mitosis Detection | `track.mitosis_learned` | tracking-lineage | Provenance the surveyor itself flags as unverified, an unmaintained Keras library, and track.divisions covers the capability at zero dependency cost. |
| Lineage Metrics | `track.lineage_metrics` | tracking-lineage | Serves a proliferation/heritability assay outside this lab's focus and its duration columns duplicate track.features. |
| Lineage Tree / TrackScheme panel | `core.lineage_tree_view` | tracking-lineage | An XL GUI panel whose only purpose is inspecting a lineage forest this lab's assays rarely produce; core.track_display buys the verification value first at a fraction of the cost. |
| Manual track curation operations | `core.track_editing` | tracking-lineage | A replayable per-element edit list has no stable key in a pull-based graph, so recorded corrections cannot survive the upstream re-run they exist to survive. |
| Population Dynamics | `motion.population_dynamics` | tracking-lineage | The part that works without lineage (count/births/losses per t) is already expressible as transform.transfer_structure track->frame plus a reducer, and the rest is a proliferation readout outside the stated focus. |
| Predictive Linker (Kalman / velocity / drift) | `track.predictive` | tracking-lineage | It is a `predictor` Mode on a linker's cost (the surveyor's own fallback) — as a standalone card it emits predicted positions that no node consumes. |
| Rose Plot | `view.rose_plot` | tracking-lineage | Its scalars (mean angle, resultant R, Rayleigh p) belong as columns on the track-features node, and the display half is the one thing the engine cannot express. |
| Track Feature Clustering | `motion.track_cluster` | tracking-lineage | Generic multivariate clustering over an exported feature matrix rather than a microscopy measurement; nodelab_v2/export.py already gets the table to a tool that does this better. |
| Tracking Accuracy Metrics (CTC) | `analysis.tracking_metrics` | tracking-lineage | Depends on an annotated CTC reference lineage this lab will never produce; analysis.spt_metrics delivers the same flow.iterate tuner loop for the assay they actually run. |
| Trajectory Plot (at common origin) | `view.trajectory_plot` | tracking-lineage | A display mode of core.track_display rather than a node — nothing is left of it once trails exist. |
| Transformer Association Tracker (Trackastra) | `track.transformer` | tracking-lineage | A fourth global linker that drags in an entire second learned runtime for a capability track.lap plus track.divisions already covers. |
| Van Hove Distribution | `motion.van_hove` | tracking-lineage | Without the CURVE domain it collapses to one non-Gaussian-parameter scalar that belongs as a column on diffusion_fit/jump_distance rather than a card of its own. |

## Appendix E - Overlap disposition (all 269)

Produced by seven sharded adversarial critics that read the **source** of the shipped nodes
each candidate collides with, not just the MANUAL. Every candidate received exactly one
verdict; coverage was 269 of 269 with no duplicates.

| Verdict | n | Share | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `distinct` | 116 | 43% | The declared overlap was superficial. Build as proposed. |
| `fold-into-mode` | 92 | 34% | Not a new node. A Mode/parameter addition to an existing node, specified in the disposition. |
| `rescope` | 57 | 21% | A real residual survives once the overlapping part is cut. Build the smaller node. |
| `subsumed` | 4 | 1% | Already reachable today by a followable route. Dropped. |

Critic confidence: 113 high, 150 medium, 6 low. The 6 low-confidence rows are the ones to
re-examine first.

**Revised program shape.** 173 new nodes (116 as proposed, 57 reduced in scope), plus **92 mode
or parameter additions to 44 existing nodes** (34 shipped, 10 that are themselves candidates
elsewhere in this table -- a secondary dedupe the blind surveys could not see), and 4 dropped.

### E.1 Subsumed - dropped (4)

| Candidate | Route that already achieves it |
|---|---|
| `mech.strain_measure` | analysis.dvc_field -> Mode('strain_type', ['infinitesimal','green-lagrange','almansi','hencky'], default='infinitesimal') (dvc_field.py:373-406), backed by kernels/aldvc_field.py:747-772 strain_from_gradient which implements all four (plus _hencky at :781). analysis.accumulate_field inherits the choice through the dvc_strain_type provenance stamp (dvc_field.py:170-174, accumulate_field.py:187) and recomputes strain from the cumulative displacement, so the incremental path gets the same four measures. |
| `flow.bake` | Place several `io.dock` nodes in the one graph, at each point you want baked — after registration, after deconvolution, after segmentation. io.dock is an ordinary graph node, not a GUI boundary: nodelab_v2/ops.py:55 registers DOCK_OP='io.dock' with a three-value state mode (DOCK_LIVE/DOCK_HELD/DOCK_DOCKED at ops.py:70), its own checkpoint-folder param and its own opaque bake record in params (ops.py:50), and MANUAL §13 states 'A Dock's state, its checkpoint folder and its bake record are ordinary node params/modes, so they are part of the structure, not the ui extras'. `live` is an identity... |
| `value.separate_xyz` | There is nothing to separate: vector-valued data in this engine is ALREADY stored as separate scalar columns/layers, because Dataset.with_attribute requires shape == axes.shape_for(domain) exactly with no trailing component axis. analysis/object_metrics.py's _OBJECT_METRIC_COLUMNS proves it — 'velocity' emits ('vy','vx') as two independent columns (object_metrics.py:26) — and analysis/dvc_field.py and dic_correlate.py emit their displacement and strain components the same way. So splitting a DVC displacement into u/v/w to plot each, or taking the z-component of migration alone, is reading the... |
| `analysis.zones_of_influence` | transform.grow_points with Grow='surface', Overlap='nearest', protect=False, distance (µm) set to the maximum territory radius you want (this is the proposal's 'exclude territory beyond a max distance'; set it larger than the field for an unbounded SKIZ), labels=<your object raster>. Output is a territory Label raster + table, µm-anisotropic in 3D under the lever. |

### E.2 Fold into an existing node (92)

Each row is a specification of an edit, not a proposal. `mode_spec` is reproduced in full
because its precision is the point -- the critics cite the actual internal constants
(`_SEGMENT_METHODS`, `_MEASURE_SHAPE`, `_OBJECT_METRIC_COLUMNS`) the edit extends.

### E.3 Rescoped - build the residual (57)

`v` shows the value score before and after the cut. Several of these stop being nodes at all
and become `_shared/` refactors; those are marked in the residual.

| Candidate | v | Becomes | What survives | Cut |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `relate.parent` | 10->8 | Relate Label Regions (parent/child) | Assigns a parent LABEL row to each child LABEL row of a SECOND label instance on the same Dataset, by child-centroid containment or maximum voxel overlap, and writes parent_id + distance_centroid_um + distance_minimum_um (child centroid to nearest parent perimeter voxel) on the child table and children_count + Mean_<col> on the parent table. ## Appendix F - Architecture corrections (88)

Three sharded critics re-derived the `fit` classification against the engine source, having
first read the 64 KB constraints brief. They were asked to find errors in **both** directions.

**Every correction made a candidate easier to build. None made one harder.**
59 were reclassified into a cheaper fit class; 29 kept their class but had their granularity,
domains or dependencies corrected. The resulting shift:

| Fit class | Before | After | Delta |
|---|---|---|---|
| `drop-in` | 150 | 201 | +51 |
| `small-core` | 61 | 41 | -20 |
| `new-core` | 56 | 27 | -29 |
| `unscored` | 2 | 0 | -2 |

The pattern is systematic, not incidental. The fit pass repeatedly mistook a *missing node*
for a *missing engine capability*, and repeatedly assumed that per-element or curve-shaped
results need a new domain. Both premises are false; see section 1.

### F.1 Reclassified cheaper (59)

The 'field expressions do not round-trip through serialize.py' claim is wrong in kind: serialize.py stores only node ids, op_keys, params, modes, zones and edges — a Field is a runtime PAYLOAD constructed by a compute at pull time from op_key+params, so there is nothing to serialize and nothing to lose. ### F.2 Class kept, declaration corrected (29)

Mostly granularity. 189 of the 269 candidates were declared `WHOLE_SERIES` by surveyors who
did not know it drops off the lazy path (`_shared/map_image.py:98-101`) into a full 6-D
realization. Note the recurring exemption the critics found: a node that reads only tables
never enters `_map_image` at all, so its granularity declaration is inert.
